## 2026-10-25 — 2D layout findings fixed (R2)

Read-only/pending operations remain guarded; stale pending targets close instead of silently placing an obsolete entity. Only geometry fields are copied into the entity.
- Medium / FIXED — Suggesting a queued parent with persisted child racks derives enclosing geometry from those children, preserving every child coordinate. There is no fallback that relocates the parent away from its children.
- Medium / FIXED — Persisted inactive geometry remains an obstacle. Suggestions, manual placement validation and displayed overlap warnings account for inactive racks/zones that remain on the diagram. The lifecycle status does not implicitly free a visible footprint.

## 2026-10-25 — 2D layout review R1 of a65ce6c

State: NEEDS_CLAUDE_FIX. Role: same implementer performing the user's requested self-review; not independent review. Production source unchanged during review.

- High / OPEN — WarehouseCanvas.tsx:510 plus WarehouseDesignerWorkspace.tsx:416: queued objects are hidden, rotation of pending objects is blocked, and the queue/tree only selects entities (palette drag data only creates new ones). If the first-fit suggestion cannot place an existing entity, there is no manual placement/resize/rotation path. Reproduction: a100x400 zone has room for the120x40 rack rotated90degrees, but the suggestion keeps it queued; the UI cannot place that existing rack. Provide explicit manual placement for queued IDs, including phone/keyboard interaction, while retaining queued state until placement.
- Medium / OPEN — layout-placement.ts:178: when a queued zone already has persisted child-rack geometry, those same children are treated as obstacles for placing their own parent. Reproduced with a child at80,100: suggestion places the zone away from the child, then keeps the child unchanged and reports it outside its zone; the resulting parent geometry can be saved. Fit/reconstruct the parent around its existing children or leave it queued with an actionable reason; do not choose a separate free area for that parent.
- Medium / OPEN — layout-placement.ts:198: suggestions ignore inactive persisted racks/zones, while the canvas still renders them. Reproduced an active queued rack placed at exactly the same coordinates/size as a visible inactive rack; collision warnings also omit the inactive rack. ## 2026-10-23 — Mobile-first camera scanning for inbound receiving and putaway

- Implementer self-verification, READY_FOR_CODEX_REVIEW for this bounded camera addition. Promoted the existing transfer camera decoder to `src/lib/camera-scan.ts` and inline viewport to `src/components/operations/scanning`; old transfer paths re-export the same implementation. No new dependency or decoder duplication. ZXing stays dynamically imported only after the user starts scanning; transfer preferences/business handlers remain feature-local.
- Added a shared inline CameraScanControl with a 44px full-width mobile action and compact desktop composition. One inbound camera owner at a time, continuous receiving/common-location scans, single accepted scan per row, permission/no-camera/HTTP fallback, retry, explicit stop, late-callback guards, hidden-tab/unmount/pending cleanup and no automatic reacquisition after saving. Changing document/destination releases the prior camera. Desktop fine-pointer row focus remains available for keyboard scanners; touch does not automatically summon its keyboard. Dialogs retain shared slim scrolling and contain touch scroll chaining. Feedback follows the viewport, and long scanned codes wrap.
- Receiving camera routes decoded text through the existing SKU/barcode matching and quantity-in-selected-unit/cap checks; it does not infer packaging or submit the receipt. Putaway uses existing canonical-label/QR/ambiguous-code validation and preserves raw scan evidence; scanning never posts stock or submits the form. Damage isolation camera only fills the existing confirmedSlotCode field, does not tick physical-separation confirmation or write inventory; its unchanged Backend slot-code/custom-barcode contract still validates on explicit submission. This change does not extend damage-isolation QR/canonical-code acceptance or auto-select receiving locations.
- GitNexus pre-change impact: decoder and inline viewport HIGH (two indexed direct callers each, one/two transfer flows); warned before edits. ReceiveQuickActions/PutawayScanBar/row confirmation LOW (one direct caller each); receipt dialog LOW (two callers). CameraScanControl/damage dialog are unindexed, so direct source/caller tracing supplemented graph results. Aggregate detect_changes includes previous uncommitted work and is not claimed to isolate this camera patch; preserved AGENTS/CLAUDE and all earlier edits. Transfer camera mocks were redirected to the new shared module without removing assertions.
- Regression: 41 files / 290 tests passed, zero failed/skipped (`../outputs/inbound-camera-final-20261023.json`), then the four affected UI suites passed all 43 tests after final focus/scroll adjustments (`../outputs/inbound-camera-ui-final-20261023.json`); these runs overlap and are not added together. Scoped ESLint, production-config TypeScript, Prettier and default-repository diff-check passed. An early receiving test failed because its status query matched both camera loading and scan results; fixed the test selector without dropping quantity/error assertions. Final-source isolated Webpack rebuild passed compilation, TypeScript and all 73 static pages. Protected AGENTS/CLAUDE and Backend configuration hashes remain unchanged; a separate autocrlf=false diagnostic reports CRLF whitespace in those pre-existing instruction edits, which were preserved.
- Browser verification used actual production components in an isolated Vite fixture, the real ZXing decoder and a generated-QR canvas MediaStream, never the user's physical camera or deployed data. At 390px, document width 390px and receipt dialog client/scroll width 358px; 44px camera control. Holding a product QR kept quantity at 1, and closing/saving ended the track. Location QR confirmed the assigned row and stopped when complete. At 320px the page stayed 320px wide; 1440px desktop retained a 768px dialog and bounded 382px camera viewport; light/dark screenshots inspected. Artifacts: `../outputs/inbound-camera-mobile-20261023.png`, `../outputs/inbound-camera-mobile-dark-20261023.png`, `../outputs/inbound-camera-desktop-20261023.png`. The existing ZXing MultiFormatReader emitted non-fatal OneDReader/NotFound warnings while decoding the synthetic QR stream; not hidden or claimed to be a clean console/performance benchmark. The picking page has detail and pick sheet active; these reads run concurrently, not serially. Separate committed-success feedback from refresh state in a future fix, retaining fresh-version/unknown-result barriers and blocking further writes until safe. Do not remove required refreshes just to shorten the spinner.
- Line picking already marks related queries stale without broad eager reload and owns one fresh pick-sheet read before the next action. Put-away already acknowledges the write separately and reloads detail + sheet concurrently; fresh-sheet/write-block guards remain. The latest pick-sheet benchmark (14→8 SELECTs, warm median2885→1715ms) measures a read handler, not the RecordTransferPick/CompleteTransferPicking/PutAwayTransferShipment POSTs.
- BE still reads task twice in put-away actor validation, posts ledger entries sequentially without invoking the ledger's available PreloadAsync, and awaits notification/realtime publishing after commit. Per-request ledger metadata caches already exist; capacity/mixed-product checks still require correct pending-aware validation. These are measured-follow-up candidates, not proof of the dominant delay or permission to bypass checks/background the work without durable delivery.

See the shared performance plan section14 for the exact flow and measurement-first next steps. Existing full-suite limitations remain as recorded below; unrelated rules/config/output changes excluded from commits.

## 2026-10-21 — Post-commit transfer review and remaining performance work

- Reviewer: Codex, a separate self-review of committed FE `3d04815` and BE `81ee8108`; not independent review. State `APPROVED_FINAL` for the bounded review: no confirmed Blocker/High finding. - Medium PERF-01 (OPEN): `src/features/transfer/hooks/use-transfer-task-assignment.ts:210,276` reads the full transfer before assignment, then awaits another full detail refresh after successful writes through `refreshTransferAfterWrite`. These are sequential reads around one/two assignment writes. The direct reader's measured warm median is still about 3.1s, so reducing duplicate invalidation alone does not remove this critical path. Prefer a compact task snapshot/version read and a scoped post-write response/read. Preserve expectedVersion/expectedStaffId, explicit reason, partial-success recovery and unknown-result reconciliation; do not simply remove concurrency checks or show stale actionable data.
- Medium PERF-02 (OPEN, BE): `GetMyWarehouseTasksQueryHandler.cs:23,58,293,309` still loads all task domains/children before applying TaskType and in-memory pagination. Push known type predicates down first; restrict relocation-only line reads and precompute receipt lookups once. A later paged read model must preserve global ordering, filtered totals/stats, export and warehouse/assignee isolation. Existing tests verify results, not a reduced SQL query budget.
- Medium PERF-03 (OPEN, BE dependency): `AssigneeLoadService.cs:58-95` still executes settings plus four parent-domain reads and up to four child reads serially after staff eligibility. Batched effective permissions remove candidate-count N+1, not these workload round trips. Measure this phase separately, then project/aggregate only required workload fields while preserving the existing ranking semantics and scope. Do not use Task.WhenAll on the shared DbContext or cache authorization as a shortcut.
- Medium PERF-04 (OPEN, BE): `GetTransferPickSheetQueryHandler.cs:15,30-34,80-90` uses the command-oriented tracked aggregate (all shipments/discrepancies), then separate product/unit and slot/rack/zone reads. Prefer a bounded no-tracking projection with joined metadata for the selected shipment, retaining all-shipment outstanding-pick accounting required by reservations, tenant/scope guards, lots and FEFO semantics. The earlier 3366-3459ms logs are historical handler observations, not a new benchmark or browser latency measurement.

## 2026-10-03 — Permission catalog final code review

- Role: Codex review-only pass over the tenant and System Admin permission catalog diff against `origin/dev`.
- State: `APPROVED_FINAL` under the repository gate because no Blocker or High finding was identified. One non-blocking Medium finding remains below.

### PERM-20261003-M1 — Medium

The tenant catalog receives only the filtered hierarchy from both role search and personal search/customized-only views. `PermissionCatalog` therefore calculates category/module totals from the visible subset and passes only those visible permission IDs to the module checkbox. For example, searching a single permission in a five-permission module renders `1/1`; clicking the checked module control removes only that one permission. The System Admin catalog already preserves a `completeCategory`/`completeGroup`, so the same interaction has different semantics between the two permission workspaces. Pass the complete grouped hierarchy alongside the visible hierarchy (or resolve each visible category/module back to its complete counterpart) for counts and module bulk selection, while continuing to render only matching rows. Add regressions for role search and personal customized/search filters.

### Verification and scope

- Focused tenant/admin catalog tests passed **24/24** across five files; current tests do not cover bulk selection after the hierarchy has been filtered, which is the gap described above.
- GitNexus compare analysis reports Medium blast radius across the expected tenant role, personal permission and System Admin role/catalog render flows. Direct source tracing confirmed save payloads, 409 handling, permission scope enforcement and unsaved-close protection are otherwise unchanged.
- `git diff --check origin/dev...HEAD` passed in both repositories.
- No application source, dependency, API write, database, migration, seed, deployment configuration, push or merge was changed by this review.

### Fix verification — PERM-20261003-M1 resolved

- The shared tenant catalog now receives both the visible filtered hierarchy and the complete hierarchy. Category/module counts, tri-state state and module bulk actions use the complete module; only matching permission rows are rendered.
- Role search and personal customized/search modes now have the same full-module semantics as the System Admin catalog. Save payloads, permission drafts, authorization and API contracts are unchanged.
- Added role and personal regressions proving a one-row filtered module still reports the complete `1/2` count and toggles both module permission IDs.
- Verification: focused access-control tests **18/18** passed; full Frontend suite **213/213** passed across 54 files; typecheck, lint and production build passed.
- GitNexus pre-change impact was LOW for each edited symbol. Final change detection is HIGH because the shared catalog intentionally participates in six tenant role/personal execution flows; no unexpected feature or API flow is included.
- Web Interface Guidelines review found no new accessibility, focus, interaction, content-overflow or motion issue in the edited components.
- No dependency, Backend source, API contract, database, migration, seed or deployment configuration was changed.

### 2026-10-04 — Put-away quantity/UOM and UI: review round 1

- Role: Codex review-only self-review; not independent approval.
- State: `NEEDS_CLAUDE_FIX`.
- **PUTAWAY-20261004-H1 / High / open / pre-existing:** `InboundPutawayDetailPage.tsx:99` creates a new command ID for each submission. Error refetch at lines 110–111 and `keepDirtyValues` at line 76 retain the allocation while adopting the server's new version. A committed partial operation whose response is lost can therefore be posted again on manual retry. Preserve the uncertain command's original ID and immutable payload, reconcile its outcome before allowing another operation, and cover this with a page/handler regression. Confirmed against `HEAD`: this defect predates the new unit workflow.
- Database-free simulation of the actual submit function and allocation helper, with mocked commit/network responses, produced two different command IDs and versions `v1`/`v2` for the same 4-carton allocation: recorded total 192 base units versus intended 96. This does not claim real API/DB reproduction.
- **PUTAWAY-20261004-M1 / Medium / open / introduced:** `putaway-units.ts:40` converts exact BigInt hundredths back to a JS number without proving the resulting quantity preserves those hundredths. Direct execution of the current helper with quantity `90071990000`, factor `1000.000001`, entered precision 0 and base precision 2 returns `90071990090071.98` instead of exact `90071990090071.99`, even though cents are below `MAX_SAFE_INTEGER` and the input fits the request limits. This affects conversion previews and remaining-allocation validation; Backend decimal checks still protect stock posting. Reject values that cannot round-trip exactly through the UI representation or retain exact hundredths through comparisons, and add the boundary regression without silent rounding.
- Focused Frontend allocation/conversion and form interaction tests passed **23/23** across two files. Whitespace checks passed. The prior full suite/typecheck/lint/build results are recorded above, not rerun in this review.
- GitNexus detected Medium blast radius across the expected form/page flows. Missing FTS indexes limit keyword search; current source and diffs corroborated the findings and covered untracked helper/tests directly.
- Real-browser responsive, native-picker keyboard/focus and FE/API end-to-end acceptance remain pending; the previous browser runtime initialization failure has not been resolved. No new live-UI pass is claimed.
- Only this review record was appended. No application source, database connection/write, migration, seed, dependency, configuration, commit or push was performed.

### 2026-10-04 — Put-away review findings: safety fixes

- Role: Codex implementing the user's authorized findings fixes and performing self-verification.
- State: `READY_FOR_FINAL_REVIEW` (not independent approval or live end-to-end acceptance).
- **PUTAWAY-20261004-H1 / resolved:** Capture receipt ID, original version, command ID and cloned allocation lines before the first request. Uncertain network/5xx outcomes retain this exact command; retry bypasses validation against refreshed remaining stock and cannot create a fresh command. Definitive initial 400/401/403/404/409/422 rejections release the snapshot for correction. A conflict after an uncertain response does not release it, because it cannot prove whether the original operation committed.
- Uncertain submissions lock product/unit/quantity/location edits, row addition/removal and cancellation, while enabling `Gửi lại an toàn`. A synchronous ref guard blocks rapid duplicate submissions. Back navigation from the form is blocked while locked; a native unload warning protects against accidental reload/tab close. Unresolved outcomes explicitly instruct staff not to move the goods again and to contact the manager for reconciliation. The snapshot is scoped to the mounted page, not persisted across forced navigation/browser restart; the warning is not a durable recovery mechanism.
- **PUTAWAY-20261004-M1 / resolved:** Exact BigInt hundredths must round-trip through both displayed decimal precision and allocation hundredths before returning a numeric preview. Fill/default input also verifies that converting micro-units back to a number preserves the exact input. Unrepresentable values are rejected rather than rounded, without dependencies or changes to the API.
- Regression coverage includes committed-response-loss retry with refreshed zero remaining stock, original receipt/version preservation, unchanged payload despite altered form values, initial definitive rejection followed by a corrected new command, uncertain retry conflict, rapid concurrent submission, unload-warning registration/cleanup, locked UI with an enabled retry action, and the reported numerical boundary plus an adjacent representable value.
- Final verification: full Frontend suite **258/258** across **59 files**; `pnpm typecheck`, `pnpm lint`, production build and `git diff --check` passed. One earlier full-suite run while build/typecheck/lint ran concurrently hit the existing 5-second timeout in `ProductInventoryPanels.test.tsx`; the full suite passed when rerun alone. No timeout/test outside this task was modified.
- Backend focused suite **37/37** passed from the existing Release assembly (`--no-build`); existing same-command replay/version/capacity/normalization protection is reused without Backend application edits in this fix pass.
- Ponytail/React/shadcn guided local refs, existing form/error/button primitives and no new dependency. GitNexus pre-edit impact was LOW for the page/form; new untracked conversion helpers were absent from the index and their callers were traced directly. Final detection remains Medium across the expected put-away form/page flows.
- Browser viewport/native-picker and deployed API end-to-end acceptance remain pending as recorded above. No application startup, database connection/write, migration, seed, configuration change, commit, push or deletion was performed.

### 2026-10-06 — Review round 1: operational inbound UI

- Reviewer: Codex, self-review of implementer commit `537cc1a`, not independent review. State: `APPROVED_FINAL` for the no-Blocker/High Frontend review criterion only; Medium findings remain and paired Backend review is `NEEDS_CLAUDE_FIX`. Source unchanged in this review.
- **Medium / FE-IN-01 — Exact-unit tolerance still sends a raw floating-point quotient.** `src/features/inbound/pages/InboundReceivingPage.tsx:162` and `src/features/inbound/components/ReceivingPage/ReceiveGoodsDialog.tsx:210` submit remaining / factor without normalizing representational noise, even though the helper accepts quantities within a precision tolerance. Read-only Node reproduction: 0.3 / 0.1 = 2.9999999999999996; request UOM precision 0 accepts this as representable, but Backend exact decimal validation rejects the payload. Normalize to the allowed UOM precision only after proving exact representability; retain the base-unit fallback for truly fractional packaging. Test both default and Receive All paths.
- **Medium / FE-IN-02 — UOM switching conflicts with inspection correction.** `src/features/inbound/components/ReceivingPage/ReceiveGoodsDialog.tsx:136` resets actual/damaged quantities to zero for all modes. The shared editor has no correction capability flag, while Backend CorrectInspectionAsync forbids changing actual received quantity. On a correction-required receipt, choosing another UOM clears the fixed actual quantity, blocks saving, and forces error-prone re-entry. Preserve the fixed received quantity across exact unit switches or disable the UOM/actual-quantity controls for this state; leave damaged classification editable. Add a correction-state regression.
- Cross-repo High BE-IN-01 also affects the full edit screen: when both Update and UpdateCode are allowed, only the full form is rendered, so changing only code can resave operational lines using live conversion metadata.
- Re-ran **344 tests across 72 files**, all passed; typecheck passed. Prior ESLint/production build passed. No live browser acceptance was performed. No database write, migration, seed, restart, remote push or source fix.

### 2026-10-14 — Transfer put-away alignment self-review round 1

- Role: implementer self-review, not independent review. State: `APPROVED_FINAL` only under the handoff rule of no proven Blocker/High; **two Medium findings remain unresolved**. This is not release/merge acceptance; browser QA and SQL Server race remain uncertified. No source fixes, commits, pushes or DB operations in this review.
- **Medium R1 — Combined plan/rules allocations can exceed shared capacity.** `src/features/transfer/utils/transfer-putaway.ts:65`: preferring a saved plan only subtracts from that item's same-slot proposal. It does not reserve shared slot capacity for other items whose rule proposals were computed before the saved plan replaced its allocation. Reproduction: rules A→T 8, B→S 8 with S free 10; saved plan A→S 8; auto-filled form becomes S=16. Backend capacity policy rejects the whole command with 409, preventing overstock but defeating automatic fill. Reserve retained plans in the shared rules context before allocating the remaining quantities; revalidate slot compatibility/capacity after merging, not just per-item proposal quantities.
- **Medium R2 — Collapsed allocations hide validation failures.** `src/features/transfer/components/TransferWork/TransferPutAwayForm.tsx:70` and `hooks/use-transfer-putaway-form.ts:requestConfirm`: errors stay in collapsed/unmounted entry controls, with no group error indicator/open-on-invalid or summary/onInvalid feedback. Collapsing a product with missing scan evidence then confirming neither opens the confirmation nor exposes an error; the button appears broken. Expand invalid groups, expose an accessible error summary and focus the first invalid field after expansion while preserving allocations.
- Evidence: isolated copy `D:/Kovia-QA/Transfer-Putaway-Review-20261014/FE`. Existing scoped regressions **63/63 passed** (`existing-regressions.json`); **2/2 new required-behavior assertions fail** (`review-reproductions.json`, temporary `TransferPutawayReviewEvidence.test.tsx` only in the copy). They reproduce S=16>10 and zero exposed alerts after rejected validation. Repository tests/source were not edited. BE handlers, shared capacity enforcement, receipt history/Good-only counters and suggestion context were inspected; no full suite/build rerun or SQL Server/browser claim in this review.

## 2026-10-18 — Warehouse overview self-review round 1

- Scope: c7ee2e1 and its BE contract d7122ec8, direct dependencies only. Reviewer is original implementer, not independent. Re-read shared/repo AGENTS.md and .rules, UI/table rules and applicable skills. State: APPROVED_FINAL under the no-Blocker/no-High review gate; two Medium findings below remain OPEN, so this is not a statement that all defects are fixed. No production source edit or commit/push in this round.
- R1 — Medium, OPEN — src/features/dashboard/pages/WarehouseOverviewPage.tsx:124-140,156-164. A cached 403 in an inactive query suppresses every tab, but header refresh only retries the current tab's data plus warehouse options. Example: expiry denied, switch to Activity, permission/assignment restored without changing permissionKey, refresh options+overview succeeds; inactive expiry error is never rechecked and Activity remains hidden. The inverse holds for a cached denied overview while on Alerts; a searched detail error may also remain outside the refreshed preview keys. Recovery must revalidate permission/scope and retry the query causing the global denial, not merely ignore 403.
- R2 — Medium, OPEN — src/features/dashboard/components/WarehouseOverview/StockAlertDialog.tsx:28-34. Dialog is opened by external panel buttons with no registered DialogTrigger and no explicit onCloseAutoFocus target. Radix modal close prevents its default restoration and focuses its missing triggerRef; Escape leaves focus on body instead of the initiating Xem tat ca button. Restore the actual opener safely or compose a registered trigger; add keyboard-focus regression coverage.
- Verification: existing dashboard feature suite 36/36 passed; BE dashboard/reporting 18/18 passed using Release --no-build isolated fixtures/translation checks. Two temporary focused diagnostics intentionally failed: opener.toHaveFocus received body, and Activity refresh called only warehouses/overview, not cached-denied expired query. Temporary diagnostic file removed after evidence was recorded; no existing tests edited. No new build/full suite/browser/HTTP/SQL integration certification.
- No deployed DB reads/writes, migration, seed, cleanup, app restart, branch switch, commit or push in this review. Existing local processes and unrelated user changes preserved. Missing shared docs/BUSINESS_RULES.md and degraded GitNexus keyword index noted; did not rebuild a large index or infer new business rules.

## 2026-10-20 — Transfer review follow-up: lost responses, concurrent changes and redundant reads

- Assigned implementer self-review; READY_FOR_CODEX_REVIEW, not independent approval. Addressed the three P2 findings and two read-efficiency items from the user-requested review. Ponytail kept existing service/query/form patterns; shadcn FieldSet/Alert and React guidance were reused, without new dependencies or custom primitives.
- Replaced mutation-counter/five-second echo suppression with an optional per-tab origin echoed by BE. Foreign and legacy/no-origin events remain visible even while a lookup/write is pending or has succeeded. Slot lookup has a read-only mutation key. Transfer writes and warehouse-task assignment attach the same in-memory tab ID; the existing UUID fallback supports insecure HTTP scanners and no ID is allocated during SSR. The header is correlation only, never authorization.
- Forms disable inputs while saving or after an unknown result. A synchronous uncertainty guard blocks automatic/manual resend; draft/submit/update mutations explicitly disable retry. Known IDs support an explicit read-only status check and navigation to the saved document; a create whose response never returned an ID opens the list in a separate tab for manual reconciliation. This does not introduce durable create idempotency or assume an absent response means a failed write. Defensive draft reset preserves edits arriving during the save and does not navigate them away.
- Plan save marks queries stale without an eager broad refetch; the workspace owns the single detail/sheet reload. Rules wait for a complete, non-fetching snapshot, do not apply during writes/reload, and consume AbortSignal so obsolete-version requests are canceled. Mandatory fresh-version reads and all BE validation remain.

Final KOVIA_ISOLATED_BUILD=true build passed, 73 static pages. Existing fixture act warning remains; intentional network-error tests log expected failures. Regression cases cover foreign changes during successful reads/writes, own echoes versus another tab, unknown-result resend blocking/status-read failure, draft edit preservation, disabled inputs, one-owner post-save fetching, rules gating/cancellation, HTTP UUID fallback and SSR.

No live response payload or sensitive log saved, no agent business API request.
- Concrete contract mismatch found by source inspection: GetCrossDockDemandQueryHandler returns null when no demand exists; API OmitNullApiResponseData omits the data field for null; crossDockService.demand parses undefined through nullable() rather than normalizing the documented absent-data state. This can misclassify a successful no-demand read as an error. Live query-error/body correlation not captured, so not asserted as the only cause of the pictured warning. Recommended follow-up: normalize absent demand to null with explicit service tests while retaining rejection of malformed nonempty responses and real HTTP errors. Warning/validation behavior deliberately not changed under this explanation-only request.

This corrects prior visual-parity claims, not an additional hover override.

Retained transfer's hover rule, ordinary foreground and inline anchors; no padding hack, forced permanent underline or tooltip. GitNexus LOW for four renderers.
